Saved the operator doctrine into GOALS/LUCI_CONVERGENCE_MODE_PROMPT.md. Verified with  (105/105 green), , LUCI CLI v0.1.0
  Interactive coding assistant for the current workspace.

Quick start
  luci                                  Start the interactive REPL
  luci "summarize this repo"            Run one prompt and exit
  luci prompt "explain src/main.rs"     Explicit one-shot prompt
  luci --resume SESSION.json /status    Inspect a saved session

Interactive essentials
  /help                                 Browse the full slash command map
  /status                               Inspect session + workspace state
  /model <name>                         Switch models mid-session
  /permissions <mode>                   Adjust tool access
  Tab                                   Complete slash commands
  /vim                                  Toggle modal editing
  Shift+Enter / Ctrl+J                  Insert a newline

Commands
  luci dump-manifests                   Read upstream TS sources and print extracted counts
  luci bootstrap-plan                   Print the bootstrap phase skeleton
  luci lucidota-status                  Print LUCIDOTA build status bars
  luci indy-brief                       Print Indy_Reads local runtime brief
  luci indy-queue [--limit N]            List Indy_Reads quiet side queue
  luci indy-reminder <title>             Queue a quiet Indy_Reads reminder
  luci model-governor                   Check resident model VRAM/loadout decision
  luci model-runner <validate|stub>      Validate local model config or write STUB receipt
  luci cohere-chat --prompt TEXT          Call Cohere Chat through LUCIDOTA receipt bridge
  luci groq-chat --prompt TEXT            Call Groq Chat through LUCIDOTA receipt bridge
  luci rete-route --text TEXT            Route one packet through deterministic RETE/bandit gate
  luci work-order <pipeline|next-actions> Import work orders with receipts
  luci template-render                    Render deterministic template and receipt
  luci slop-audit                         Audit Blueprint-First/PocketFlow hygiene
  luci knowledge [--query TERM]           Validate/query learned knowledge library
  luci ncnn-probe                         Probe ncnn edge-runtime candidate
  luci lane-gate route --text TEXT         Route/cache fastlane-slowlane metadata packets
  luci operate --text TEXT                 Route operator input through LUCI MOA/hyperplex lanes
  luci moa-worker --absurd-dir DIR         Execute queued LUCI MOA chain nodes
  luci flow run <flow-dir> --batch-data DATA --run-id ID
                                        Run PromptFlow eval batch via LUCIDOTA receipt bridge
  luci flow batch --dag FLOW --eval DATA --run-id ID
                                        Alias for PromptFlow batch lane
  luci cockpit                          Print one-screen LUCIDOTA cockpit
  luci lucidota-survey <target>          Survey URL/file into local CAS + Postgres
  luci diogenes-smoke                   Run the CKDOG1 gRPC baby-smoke bridge
  luci agents                           List configured agents
  luci skills                           List installed skills
  luci system-prompt [--cwd PATH] [--date YYYY-MM-DD]
  luci login                            Start the OAuth login flow
  luci logout                           Clear saved OAuth credentials
  luci init                             Scaffold CLAW.md + local files

Flags
  --model MODEL                         Override the active model
  --output-format FORMAT                Non-interactive output: text or json
  --permission-mode MODE                Set read-only, workspace-write, or danger-full-access
  --dangerously-skip-permissions        Skip all permission checks
  --allowedTools TOOLS                  Restrict enabled tools (repeatable; comma-separated aliases supported)
  --version, -V                         Print version and build information

Slash command reference
Slash commands
  Tab completes commands inside the REPL.
  [resume] = also available via luci --resume SESSION.json

Core flow
  /help                                          Show available slash commands [resume]
  /status                                        Show current session status [resume]
  /compact                                       Compact local session history [resume]
  /model [model]                                 Show or switch the active model
  /permissions [read-only|workspace-write|danger-full-access] Show or switch the active permission mode
  /cost                                          Show cumulative token usage for this session [resume]

Workspace & memory
  /config [env|hooks|model|plugins]              Inspect Claw config files or merged sections [resume]
  /memory                                        Inspect loaded Claw instruction memory files [resume]
  /init                                          Create a starter CLAW.md for this repo [resume]
  /diff                                          Show git diff for current workspace changes [resume]
  /version                                       Show CLI version and build information [resume]
  /teleport <symbol-or-path>                     Jump to a file or symbol by searching the workspace

Sessions & output
  /clear [--confirm]                             Start a fresh local session [resume]
  /resume <session-path>                         Load a saved session into the REPL
  /export [file]                                 Export the current conversation to a file [resume]
  /session [list|switch <session-id>]            List or switch managed local sessions

Git & GitHub
  /branch [list|create <name>|switch <name>]     List, create, or switch git branches
  /worktree [list|add <path> [branch]|remove <path>|prune] List, add, remove, or prune git worktrees
  /commit                                        Generate a commit message and create a git commit
  /commit-push-pr [context]                      Commit workspace changes, push the branch, and open a PR
  /pr [context]                                  Draft or create a pull request from the conversation
  /issue [context]                               Draft or create a GitHub issue from the conversation

Automation & discovery
  /bughunter [scope]                             Inspect the codebase for likely bugs
  /ultraplan [task]                              Run a deep planning prompt with multi-step reasoning
  /goals [objective]                             Run a persistent-goal kicker with completion and blocked audits (aliases: /goal)
  /debug-tool-call                               Replay the last tool call with debug details
  /plugin [list|install <path>|enable <name>|disable <name>|uninstall <id>|update <id>] Manage Claw Code plugins (aliases: /plugins, /marketplace)
  /agents                                        List configured agents [resume]
  /skills                                        List available skills [resume]

Resume-safe commands: /help, /status, /compact, /clear [--confirm], /cost, /config [env|hooks|model|plugins], /memory, /init, /diff, /version, /export [file], /agents, /skills
Examples
  luci --model opus "summarize this repo"
  luci --output-format json prompt "explain src/main.rs"
  luci --allowedTools read,glob "summarize Cargo.toml"
  luci --resume session.json /status /diff /export notes.txt
  luci agents
  luci /skills
  luci login
  luci init, INDY_READS BRIEF
